### D. Older technical deliveries — administratively complete, NOT owner-accepted

**Owner decision 2026-10-31:** Older-than-seven-days original delivery/review backlog may be retired **administratively**, provided new independently approved corrections or fresh phone FAIL remain open. **AGE-ARCHIVED ≠ USER-ACCEPTED**. All current architecture, copy, mobile display standards and future regression checks are still binding under R1–R8 and their relevant feature sections.

| Group | Archived delivery, not an inferred phone PASS |
| --- | --- |
| **Public Hero/Equipment COST, conversion, effect Frames** | Original proof inventory, shared Frame, Hero-cost, conversion, Equipment-trigger and integrated review delivery (historically W1–W6); retain real material, owner, card identities, exact skill semantics and privacy (§R4, §10.9). |
| **Mobile HUD/Inspector/selection and optional response panels** | Historical delivered room-expiry trace, four fixed Equipment slots, equipment-card arrival, optional Retaliation, private Hand inspection and ordinal/Info follow-ups; retain their final rules (§R5–R6, §§10.7, 10.13, 10.17). |
| **Cross-view history, draw and skill interactions** | Historical delivered viewer rebase, Stargazing drag, Diao Chan generated Duel, Beauty result and early Steal waiting/settlement presentation; preserve independently accepted results and remaining normative behavior (§R2–R7, §§10.10–10.15). |

**Do not revive these old implementation task codes or screenshots.** Detailed original IDs, CI, exact source steps and rejected layouts remain at [the previous Git revision](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/996564b9c60d86477a242ed8da113d14c5d95fb5/docs/UX2-refine.md). Fresh failures are reviewed as a **new evidence-backed scope**; Table B's pending reviews are not closed by this age rule.

### E. Status and design precedence

1. **Authoritative current order:** this index + latest explicit owner verdict → canonical reusable **R1–R8** → specific latest approved feature exceptions. Current server rules, actual code and authenticated proof must be checked for implementation; archived instructions and old screenshots **never** override present decisions.
2. **One design, one owner:** No second task for a shared picker, H4/PAC or private Hand presentation that already has a canonical contract. A card-specific outcome gets only its distinct **source-defined semantics and acceptance**, never a copied generic implementation plan.
3. **Preserve history without bloat:** retain the most useful original anchor/ID for open scope or external references, not a chain of every patch suffix. Prior full records remain in Git. Accepted and age-archived items are not executable queues.
4. **File ownership:** Planner updates design and explicit owner acceptance only. Coding Agent owns code/tests/CI/deploy and `HANDOVER.md`. UX3 Plan B is unapproved; **new 7/8-player UX work and its old review queue have been removed**, although game engine and already-started legacy compatibility remain intact.

### Agent coordination and review — existing project workflow

The Coding Agent may implement **only** currently authorized Table A scopes, one bounded task at a time, using the newest remote `ux-v2` design and its own current `HANDOVER.md`; Table B is **owner review**, C/D are closed or archived. When Table A is empty **no development task is authorized**. Agent work is code, focused regressions, required exact-SHA CI/deployment and handoff; Planner independently designs/reviews, records owner real-iPhone PASS/FAIL and updates the approved design. Neither deployment nor passing browser tests equals phone acceptance.

An unresolved choice becomes **NEEDS USER DECISION** only for that dependent design; do not invent a game rule, bypass privacy or turn archived designs into execution. At acceptance, record the exact reviewed boundary **and condense the closed item's old implementation notes in the same change** (Planner Guide §6B). Local UI/browser testing is **five minutes total per bounded task**, not per scenario; preserve required unit/API/privacy and CI checks separately. The latest accepted six-player new-room gate, shared Negation Plan A and UX3 future Plan B remain distinct authorities.

## R. Reusable UX2 contracts — canonical patterns for future features

These patterns are **CURRENT product design**, not old implementation steps and not an automatically executable task list. Specific approved card/skill exceptions in original sections override a generic example; server rules always remain authoritative. New UX work should **reuse these shared contracts and name only the necessary variant**, rather than copy whole renderer, picker and phone matrices.

### R0. Design reuse and precedence — one source per concern

**Choose the shared solution first:** `CurrentAction` = private legal decisions (R1); public proof = true events (R1); **H4** = committed past, **PAC** = proven next step, **Local Console** = my action (R2); **one physical-card Journey** (R3); **one card-backed skill/equipment Frame** (R4); **one nonmodal choice shell** (R5); **one current Seat/Inspector/equipment grammar** (R6); **one recipient-private new-card arrival** (R7). Reuse a single implementation/presentation owner; no second banner, overlay, result-card renderer or duplicated picker for a new card type.

**Only specify a variant where the game is different:** whether a Negation blocks Steal's **gain**, Dismantle's **discard**, Borrowed Sword's **forced Attack/Weapon consequence**, Harvest's **current recipient's pick**, or a delayed Judgement. Keep original actor/target/physical identity and actual server-owned outcome. **The owner has now also explicitly accepted the shared Negation and Dismantle full flow.** Individual different card mechanics still require correct source-defined behavior; do not invent separate phone-test evidence for them. An accepted component is reusable by default; add a new task only for a measured deviation and explicitly approved fix.

**Source and status precedence:** server/game law > latest explicit owner design decision > reusable common contract > card-specific addition; **specific exceptions do not rewrite the generic shared component without approval**. Accepted = owner phone PASS in an actual named scope; age-archived = retired technical backlog with no invented PASS; pending = a real remaining phone review. Preserve best-known original section anchors only for references, and find older suffixes in Git.

### R1. Server authority and truthful public proof
**`CurrentAction` alone grants the viewer legal Play/Respond/Pass/Select/Skip**; client visuals cannot reconstruct target legality. **`PresentationSnapshot` / `PresentationClientView` supply proven PUBLIC event facts**, including actual actor/target/root/interaction/checkpoint/event IDs, sequence, true physical IDs and outcome. Anonymous/private Hand identities, hidden roles, uncommitted Negation intents, private choice/card acquisition stay viewer-scoped and never leak via H4/PAC/Inspector or TEST SWITCH. Missing/mismatched proof **fails closed**; no guessed animation, card, actor, damage or resolution. Preserve exact server card conservation and stale/replay checks.

### R2. Three information surfaces — one shared H4/PAC/Console design and approved copy

**One owner for each concern.** H4 is a **server-confirmed PUBLIC past fact**; PAC is the **proven PUBLIC current or possible next step**; the Local Dock Console gives only the authenticated viewer's **private authorized action**. None may obtain its authority from another surface's text or a guessed timer/DOM state. Public copy uses real **PLAYER names** resolved from proven IDs (Hero/skill titles are different), actual actor/target/effect and true event order.

**Universal H4 completeness — ORIGINAL unfinished task §10.59 (owner 2026-11-15 22:54 UTC):** H4 must never be *missing because a genuine publicly reportable committed action/result used a different gameplay branch, lost its public proof in a transition, was filtered by an incomplete category whitelist, or had no common presentation owner*. For **ALL supported public plays, Hero/Equipment activations, effects, counters, Judgements, settlements and public state changes**, one validated, ordered server event must have one H4 narration/owner; H4 displays the latest actually committed fact, superseded only by a genuine newer event as specified in §10.41. **Every publicly reportable actual change to HP or cards** must be included in the corresponding H4 result: HP lost as a cost, HP lost to damage, HP recovered, real draws, discards, gains, hand-to-hand transfers, Equipment/Judgement/Hand/Discard zone changes and their exact true counts, actor and affected players. When a single effect commits multiple changes, one readable factual H4 includes all important changes; when independent events arrive together or during polling, preserve public order and show them sequentially or in a truthful bounded composite rather than silently dropping one. No permanent sticky old H4, no duplicate H4, no blocking the game for display.

**Outcome-first shared copy:** Prefer the **actual committed outcome** with source skill/card context, not a bare `used [skill]` or `resolved effect`. True skill HP cost is **not damage**; requested `draw 2` is not proof of 2 drawn; avoided damage is not HP loss; recovery can be capped. Examples only when independently committed: `Test Player 2 used Self Sacrifice: lost 1 HP and drew 2 cards.`; `Test Player 3 recovered 1 HP.`; `Test Player 4 drew 2 cards.`; `Test Player 1 discarded 1 card from Test Player 2.`. If no HP/card movement genuinely occurred, explain the committed counter, prevention, redirect, Judgement or phase/action outcome instead; never fabricate deltas, hidden identities or generic H4 filler. The screen-reader text must convey the same public numbers. Keep accepted specific grammar for Fanjian/Negation/Harvest, real physical CardFace and original ownership; a generic contract must *feed*, not destroy, their proven richer presentations. The **entire H4 pipeline** is reusable, not just one named function. See original §10.59, now USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED 2026-11-19 23:32 UTC; its single shared authoritative H4 contract remains effective.

| Surface | What is allowed | Example canonical copy |
| --- | --- | --- |
| **H4 — one upper-positioned factual event line** | Only the latest committed real play, counter cancellation/restoration, confirmed damage/prevention, skill outcome or settlement, with exact public proof. **Never** `Waiting`, `Negation can...`, `Another Negation can...`, `currently`, `again` or a duplicate `EFFECT TRIGGERED` toast. | `King played Duel on Test Player 4.`; `Test Player 3's Negation cancels King's Duel effect on Test Player 4.`; `Test Player 2 counters Test Player 3's Negation. The Duel effect on Test Player 4 is restored.` |
| **PAC — one lower PUBLIC activity/next-step capsule** | Actual proven turn owner or next public actor, active public response window, or a **possible** further counter **only while server permits it**. Never private intents/Pass, hidden responder/card ownership, hidden count, guessed phase or settlement. | `Test Player 2's turn`; `Waiting for counter responses`; `Negation can restore the Duel effect on Test Player 4.`; `Waiting for Test Player 3 · Duel`. For secret all-seat windows: `Waiting for Negation responses`. If proof is absent: `Resolving…` or hide. |
| **Local Console — private decision** | The genuine viewer's own server-authorized `CurrentAction`: Play/Select/Confirm/Pass/Skip, selection count or personally submitted lock. Other viewers never see it. | `Select 1 card and 2 Duel participants`; `You passed. Waiting for the other players.` only after the viewer's lock is server confirmed. |

**H4 geometry and lifetime:** one horizontal centre in the measured safe battlefield (not tied to a moving card DOMRect), normal readable 14–16px where feasible, **natural 3+ line height without clipping**, same x/width across polling, texts, countdown and TEST SWITCH at equal geometry. On a safely unobstructed ~440 CSS-px four-player stage, **320–340px middle lane, normally 340px**, rather than artificially 258px. H4 may visually cover a true small author-PARKED miniature when the later approved safe priority permits it. During a genuine public CardFace **flight**, the moving card may briefly cross H4 with H4 legible above, but **stationary** public CardFace never overlaps H4; the current fixed-upper rule is §10.45. Protect full original card art, touches, Inspector, chooser, other real Seats and legal controls. The latest unsuperseded genuine fact stays until its bounded display/true next public event or stage progression; old root/Negation text must retire at genuine handoff, never replay after turn resumes or vanish forever due only to timing/layout collision.

**PAC geometry and clock priority (owner 2026-11-03 22:38):** one stable lower-centred position **above/outside the Dock and with its lower outer border visually TOUCHING the Local Console guidance panel's upper outer border**, compact and readably wide (~340 CSS px on safe 440px phone; 320–340 when protected bounds require). Longer copy grows **upward**, not to an upper/side candidate when choice phases or TEST SWITCH change; countdown **yields to PAC**, with compact far-right readable clock roughly **36–42px** or safe right-edge vertical relocation where real geometry requires. PAC may overlap PARKED small cards but **not** active enlarged REVEAL, obstruct the Inspector/actor picker or cover countdown/actions. In supported compact 4/5/6-player rooms, measure actual protected obstacles; do not force a 340px box through them. Legacy larger-room save compatibility is not authorization for new 7/8-player UI work. If protected placement is impossible, use a bounded recovery/pending state, not invisible/illegible text or invented public semantics.

**Accessibility and lifecycle:** one polite non-spamming live message per actually new public fact, no duplicated announcements/upper-right generic notice or stale text after stage transitions; appropriate `aria-live`, reduced motion, readable contrast and interaction pass-through. A viewer's private decision or local stale timer cannot move public PAC/H4, alter another viewer's semantics or restart a fact's lifetime. **A private button labelled `TAKE DAMAGE` is a legal decline of defense**, possibly causing actual damage; it does **not** prove HP loss, since armor, skills or other effects may prevent it. A public damage result/H4 such as `[player] took 1 damage.` requires authentic committed damage proof, never merely a Skip/timeout or tap. Negation Pass/skill Skip are not TAKE DAMAGE.

**Global PAC-to-Console seam contract:** Whenever a *separate visible PAC* exists, its measured bottom edge and the measured actual Console guidance top edge must meet (target 0px visual gap, tolerance ≤2 CSS px including border rounding), without overlapping Console text, controls or any real hit target. Anchor to the current measured Console DOMRect, **not** the battlefield/table bottom minus a fixed inset. Longer PAC copy grows upward while its lower edge stays fixed; PAC remains bottom-anchored, while the later §10.45 upper H4 and its genuine stationary CardFace use a separate locked measured top/centre and **must not move H4 down** to consume spare height. The accepted ≥8px full-card/PAC separation, opponent Seats, clock, responsive compact split, viewer privacy and atomic H4/PAC lifetime still govern. If even the safe compact version cannot fit, report real Rects; never silently leave a large gap or cover the Dock. When the target-choice status is intentionally merged into a private chooser and separate PAC is absent, do not render a duplicate just to satisfy this geometry assertion.

Use these canonical typography/position/ownership rules for ALL Steal, Dismantle, Duel, Harvest, Hero/Equipment and AOE display. **Card-specific H4/PAC semantics belong in §10.25, not separate renderer designs.**

### R3. One real physical CardFace and viewer-relative history

A publicly displayed physical card must be backed by one true server-committed **physicalCardId**, event/root, actor/material owner, disclosure and destination; it has **one visual owner**, not a duplicate full-size/static/legacy copy or hidden identity. Played Attack/Dodge/Negation/Duel response cards park in chronological history at **their true author Seat/Dock** relative to each viewer (TOP vertical, sides inward, local above Dock), with only real global committed response ordinals; other Equipment/Judgement/Hand-transfer outcomes follow their actual physical zones. TEST SWITCH/resize/reconnect reprojects genuine public parking without replaying old viewer travel. **§3 alone defines the shared Policy-B visible timing, interruption and retirement; §10.41 handles later independent event recency.** H4/PAC/Console belong to R2; real owner-private receipts belong to R7.

### R4. Reusable real-card-backed Hero/Equipment COST, CONVERSION and EFFECT frame
Use **one compact neutral/antique-gold frame** with actual printed Hero/skill/Equipment label **ABOVE genuine public physical CardFace(s)**, one factual H4 explanation and no second same-event notice. For one paid card show one genuine image; two side by side; three or more in a contained inspectable strip/grid without hiding a paid card. Server-proven `activationOwnerId`, `materialOwnerId`, true `physicalCardId`, source/recipient/root/proven outcome and public disclosure must agree. A paid target-owned card parks with the **target/material owner**, not necessarily the activator. AS ATTACK / AS DODGE / AS PEACH / AS OVERINDULGENCE conversions keep **original card art and suit/rank** plus proven `AS` semantics, never a counterfeit converted face or invented play. On no safely public physical card, use a **text-only** proven outcome; private draw/replacement remains concealed. No generic Deflection redirect assumption for unrelated skills. Covers 30 Standard Heroes/46 skills, 9 Weapons, 2 Armors, 2 Mounts; official game semantics are sourced from repository rules. See §10.9.

### R5. One safe choice-panel shell, independent data authority
`BattlefieldChoiceStage` is a **compact nonmodal battlefield-safe shell**, never a second game-rule engine. Public Harvest pool uses only server-revealed true CardFaces; Steal/Burning Bridges chooser uses authorized **private actor-only keys** (concealed opponent Hand stays anonymous; genuinely public Equipment/Judgement preserves identity). Only one active eligible row; if multiple nonempty zones use HAND/EQUIPMENT/JUDGEMENT tabs, retaining selection across tabs. Horizontal swipe for overflow, real public Info separately from selection, compact fixed action footer, server-authorized Confirm/Use/Skip only; repeated tap can deselect where permitted. Spectators can SWITCH/Inspect but never choose or see private candidates. Safe interactive controls, transparent pass-through outside the frame; avoid full-window input shields, H4/Hand/timer/Seat overlaps, forced page scrolling. **Retaliation and other special pickers use same shell vocabulary, not automatic copy of different action semantics.** See §10.17; §10.22 is a short accepted cross-reference to the same picker.

### R6. Supported-phone Seat, Inspector, Hand and Equipment invariants
**Current new-room product scope is 4/5/6 players only** (§10.24.1); **5 players = TOP4 genuine opponents, 6 = TOP5**, all using the accepted viewer-relative clockwise ordering. No new 7/8-player phone acceptance, SIDE-layout repair or thumbnail parking task is required/authorized. Stable SERVER player/Seat IDs, not DOM position, determine identity, source/target, Inspector, selection and PUBLIC CardFace author parking. Use measured safe phone geometry, actual full-width Hero portrait art, equal-width TOP frames, safe readable PLAYER name/HP/Equipment and accessible real target vs Info/SWITCH hit areas.

The single compact nonmodal Inspector follows the **actual selected Hero**, includes all genuine legible skills and public zones, with real portrait LEFT; Hero name and HP RIGHT first row, actual PLAYER name and true Hand count RIGHT second row, and **Judgement 2 slots bottom LEFT / Equipment 4 bottom RIGHT** with equal-size miniatures. Older upper-right Judgement layout is superseded. Own-Hand inspection-only focus cannot submit gameplay; selected-card DETAILS belongs inside the genuine card. Permanent Equipment slots are **Weapon / Armor / +1 Horse / −1 Horse**, recognizable diagonal sword/shield/+1/−1, never counterfeit; permanent equipped miniature appears only after true card arrival. Protect disclosure, responsive accessibility, and genuine author-attached parked cards.

**Historical only:** Earlier approved larger-player mapping and eight-player SIDE frame remain in [immutable design history](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/b6b8e9b852ca115cf36d92760654d2ce33c6254a/docs/UX2-refine.md) and existing saved-game compatibility; no 7/8 task queue or unreviewed phone gate survives this owner scope change.

### R7. Privacy-safe public skill outcomes and private acquired-card display
A public Hero skill without a genuine paid PUBLIC CardFace (e.g. Beauty) uses **one proof-backed H4 fact-only announcement**, upgraded in place on proven final outcome. Example: `Diao Chan used Beauty and drew 1 card.` only when public `DRAWN` proof confirms one card; never show card suit/rank/name to observers or duplicate ACTIVATE/DRAWN toasts. A genuine private acquire/draw/steal keeps original physical identity but **shows the real card once to the authorized recipient only**, then settles into Hand, with no visible `PRIVATE DRAW` heading or old transfer replay after TEST SWITCH. See §10.14 and §10.25.2 ARCH-02.

**All public HP/card outcome counts; never private identities:** H4 must report *every actual publicly disclosable HP/card operation*, including standard Draw Phase and Discard Phase, ordinary card/equipment effects, active/triggered Hero skills, Peach/healing, HP costs, failed/prevented damage, physical discards/zone changes, transfers, post-Dying continuation and publicly known reward draws. A private acquisition can be public as a **count** when game rules/server proof establish that count; it does **not** make the recipient's hidden faces/IDs/suits/ranks or submitted intents public. Initial secret setup and viewer-private inspection/selection are NOT public gameplay changes, and a hidden action with no public disclosure authority cannot be inferred. No requested provider amount, local HP/Hand diff, `@history` sentence or private draw event alone proves the public count. Publish only committed amounts from a verified server result; reveal a cost/HP change when committed, then defer a pending draw/recovery until actually applied. A Dying/death path must never claim unexecuted draws. Retain idempotency, event-root identity, public chronology and one H4 message ownership across player switches, queued events and refresh.

### R8. Required acceptance and test boundary
Normative acceptance always checks truthful server event, original physical identity, actual author/recipient, real legal actions and hidden-data protection; **user iPhone PASS is distinct from green CI/deploy**. For Coding Agent, prefer **one focused local UI/browser regression, cumulative ≤5 minutes per bounded task**, with fast targeted unit/API/privacy and mandatory existing CI as required; do not rebuild historic viewport × Hero × response matrices. For this review, an older technically delivered item may be **administratively age-closed** only by explicit owner decision, clearly labelled **NOT OWNER ACCEPTED**, while newer phone-review tasks remain open in Table B.

## 1. Authority, gameplay and privacy

1. **Server authority:** Real rules, attack legality, Duel continuation, Negation eligibility, damage, deadlines and card conservation remain unchanged. `CurrentAction` alone controls private selectable Hand cards, legal providers, Confirm, Cancel, Skip and End. Never reconstruct legal actions in React.
2. **Public event authority:** `PresentationV2`, `PresentationSnapshot` and validated public events/proofs determine genuine public card identity, played-as conversion, actor, target, committed order and settlement. Do not derive identity or causality from seat index, player name, HP change, latest animation, inferred Hand composition or merely observing a private selection. A private selected card **must not** become a public reveal.
3. **Privacy:** An opponent's Hand content, private response opportunities, hidden roles, anonymous target-card identities and skill options stay secret. Public Hand **count**, public Equipment, public Judgment and public skills may be displayed where permitted. A host may control only server-authorized test players, not take over a human participant.
4. **Seat ownership:** Actual opponent Seats and the Local Player Dock stay fixed. A public card parks in front of its **server-proven actor's** physical Seat/Dock on *each viewer's* screen; source and target are facts, never inferred from geometry.
5. **One public copy:** A committed card may change visible position/scale while transitioning but must not be simultaneously duplicated by a legacy animation, old Stage and new presenter. Any public information missing validation **fails closed**, with a readable neutral waiting state when needed, never a fictitious CardFace or an incorrect source/target.
6. **No gameplay blocking:** Presentation may not delay or stretch authoritative response windows, turn progression, damage, card submission or network updates. Timers continue from the server deadline.
7. **One-screen interaction:** The player must still see their Hand, Skills, response Guidance, timer and legal buttons. Public card animation is *informational only*; card reveal/parking must never steal interaction focus or intercept touches.

## 2. Battlefield layout — iPhone-first

The screen has three stable areas:

| Area | Responsibility | Placement |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Physical opponent Seats | Who is at the table; HP, public information, available test-player Switch | Fixed top-row or side-column topology |
| **Central battlefield** | One genuine active public CardFace and server-proven H4; PAC independently describes the next public step | Shared measured fixed-upper H4 and H4-aware stationary full CardFace centre per §10.45, safely separated from PAC, Seats and controls |
| Local Player Dock | The viewer's Hero, Skills, Equipment, private Hand and `CurrentAction` commands | Fixed at bottom; never displaced by a reveal |

### 2.1 True public CardFace centre — measured H4-aware layout

One genuine PUBLIC server-committed CardFace retains its original readable art, suit, rank and full name. Its **stationary** full-size centre is based on the actual measured **fixed upper H4**, current PAC, Seat, Dock, Inspector and legal-choice obstacles under **§10.45**; it is **not** the visual/table midpoint or an old per-card Attack/Duel 60×90 experiment. On the reviewed reference phone, ~90×135 CSS px is a legible normal public face where real geometry permits. Stationary H4/CardFace overlap is forbidden; a genuine direct physical transfer may briefly cross H4 **during motion only** (§10.42). The separate H4 says a truthful public fact, e.g. `[player] played Attack on [target].`, using actual authenticated public actor/target identifiers. One authentic central result owner; no second Hero/target illustration, duplicate card or obstruction of PAC/Console.

### 2.2 Seat-front public card thumbnails

- After the true centre reveal, park **the same original card** as a legible compact 2:3 miniature at its real author/zone. **Measure its actual footprint** instead of assuming a former ~48×72 or ~50×75 size when anchoring H4; see §10.45.
- **Opponents in top row:** place the thumbnail **just below their actual Hero Seat**, clearly associated with that player without covering HP, name, Hand count, VIEW or SWITCH.
- **Side-column opponents:** place beside the Seat's **battlefield-facing inward edge**.
- **Local player:** place the card **immediately above the Local Dock**, on the battlefield side, not inside the private Hand or on top of Guidance/Confirm/Skip.
- Derive anchor rectangles from the actual DOM Seat/Dock identified by server player ID. Recompute safely for actual measured 4/5/6-player phone Seat/Dock geometry and resize; previously started legacy rooms remain truthful without adding new 7/8 mobile visual work. When full size cannot fit, use contained compact layout or a small verified recent-card count. Never relocate a physical Seat to create space.
- For **ordinary Attack/Dodge and non-Duel interactions**, show the latest public card for each involved player, with at most one recent subdued predecessor as space allows; older cards may be summarized. **Exception: an active Duel retains the real Duel root and every committed Attack in full, grouped at their true author's Seat/Dock with topology-dependent orientation and a cross-player chronological sequence index (§8.1).** No latest-per-duelist replacement or `+N` substitute; never create a card without a genuine public commit.

### 2.3 Visible emphasis

- A current public card/recent newly parked card may have a restrained gold/red/blue outline based on *card or result emphasis*, not a route or link.
- Pending **Attack against the current viewer** gives the **existing Local Dock response/Guidance panel a red-accented border and readable instruction**; the entire screen should not become a blocking red modal. When the legal response ends, this border promptly retires.
- Target selection uses a normal selected Seat accent; settled outcomes belong adjacent to the **true affected player's Seat/Dock** as short labels. Colour always has accompanying readable text or an icon.
- **Hide the persistent DECK and DISCARD piles entirely from the normal battlefield (not merely shrink or move them).** Preserve real draw/discard data, card lifecycle, event animation destinations and any dedicated legal pile interaction; see §8.2.

## 3. The one public-card presentation lifecycle

For each newly **server-confirmed, visible public played-card event**:

1. **REVEAL — accepted Policy B:** A genuinely server-committed public physical CardFace normally remains readable at the measured H4-aware centre for **up to 4,000ms from the first truly visible large frame**. A **new server-committed public response/counter in the same interaction** takes centre priority immediately, retiring the former centre to genuine history. A **new independent committed public play** also supersedes an older *final/fact-only result* that no longer owns the active public event (§10.41, e.g. Fanjian FINAL → genuine Attack/Steal). Distinguish this from mere same-root settlement/damage updates, polls, private decisions, uncommitted responses, Pass or deadline ticks: those cannot cut short an otherwise valid physical reveal or replay an expired fact. Every new legitimate face has one original physical ID and the correct event. The approved Fanjian direct journey retains its specific ~0.3/3.4/0.3s motion (§10.42).
2. **MOVE:** On valid expiry or committed-response interruption, normally park the same physical ordinary play/response card at its true **author's Seat/Dock** in about **380ms**, without delaying the newer committed face. Genuine Equipment, Judgement and Hand-transfer cards instead follow their actual proven **material destination**, including accepted direct Fanjian movement; never force all cards to an author-Hand path. One original card, no two simultaneous full-size centre faces, ghost/card duplicates or connectors.
3. **PARK/WAIT:** For ordinary public reactions, keep the public thumbnail at the genuine author's Seat/Dock while waiting. **Duel exception:** preserve the full committed-card history *split by the actual author*, stacked vertically below top Seats, extended inward horizontally at left/right Seats, and horizontally above the local player's Dock (§8.1). Every card carries its real cross-player order index. Never show an uncommitted response. The Local Dock alone presents `CurrentAction`.
4. **RESPONSE:** A later genuinely committed public response gets its **own** centre reveal and then parks before **its own author**, independent of who is currently the viewer.
5. **SETTLEMENT:** Show a **server-proven** blocked/damage/negated/otherwise resolved result close to the affected real Seat/Dock. Initial result readability target **~2 seconds**; next independent proven action supersedes old display promptly. Keep the author cards long enough to understand the result; no animation can hold up gameplay.
6. **RETIRE:** Retire expired/superseded public physical and factual owners without an old H4/card ghost or replay. Only the genuinely newer server-committed card or eligible independent new public play under §3/§10.41 takes over, never a mere poll, pending response, timeout, private choice or guessed future action. Server deadlines and current legal operations continue independently.

**Identity/order:** Server event/root/interaction/actor/physical IDs and real zones determine one renderer owner (§R3). Poll/reconnect/TEST SWITCH never restart or prematurely expire an already accepted reveal. Do not queue genuine committed responses behind an older 4s timer, or allow a Fanjian fact-only FINAL to obscure a newer real play (§10.41). When several valid events arrive together, preserve the true committed order, one physical card each and available legal controls.

**Reduced motion:** Keep one genuine readable full-size static centre card, without translation/scale movement, and retire it on the **same actual approved Policy-B/new-public-event ownership rules**. Settle to the true zone/Seat without animated travel, preserve accessible H4 and original deadlines.

**Zero graph elements:** The new public card presenter must never render SVG relationship connectors, coloured authored/effect strokes, arrows, line branches or graph-only card nodes. Implement geometry for *positioning moving card sprites*, not for representing relationships.

## 4. Authoritative reference flows

### 4.1 Ordinary Attack → Dodge from both viewpoints

**State A: Local attacker commits Attack.** Before Confirm, only the attacker's private selected Hand card/target selection is visible. After authoritative submission, show the real Attack **centre at Hand size** to viewers for whom it is public; message `Cao Cao used Attack on Zhao Yun`. After its normal 4s or a genuine new committed response interrupt (§8.7), shrink/park it **above Cao Cao's Dock** in Cao Cao's view, or **below Cao Cao's real opponent Seat** in other players' views. The legitimate target and responder are not deduced by watching the animation.

**State B: Defender sees incoming Attack.** In Zhao Yun's viewpoint, the newly visible genuine Attack first appears **in the centre**, accompanied by `Cao Cao used Attack on you`. It parks when the default hold ends or a real committed response interrupts, at **Cao Cao's true opponent Seat**, not at Zhao Yun. Zhao Yun's existing Local Dock response/Guidance acquires a red accented border and a clear short message, e.g. `Cao Cao attacked you. Play Dodge or Skip.` Show the real response timer and only the options offered by `CurrentAction`. A normal Attack is **not** generally playable as Dodge; physical Attack `AS DODGE` (Zhao Yun Braveheart/Longdan) appears only where the server genuinely allows conversion. Do not reveal a privately selected Dodge or invent a Dodge placeholder.

**State C: Defender confirms Dodge.** After server confirmation, the newly committed Dodge immediately supersedes any still-revealing Attack (§8.7). The publicly played genuine Dodge (or an actually proven physical Attack-as-Dodge with correct displayed physical art and played-as label) is shown **centre-first at Hand size** for each viewer. Message `Zhao Yun played Dodge`. Then shrink/park it **above Zhao Yun's Dock** in Zhao Yun's own view, or **beneath Zhao Yun's Hero Seat** in Cao Cao's and observers' views. Keep the older Attack thumbnail at Cao Cao's seat as subdued context until the blocked result is readable. No counter-effect line.

**State D1: Attack blocked.** When server-verified, show `DODGED` / `Attack blocked` as the true public result near the defender Seat, with authentic Attack/Dodge history before retirement. The private `TAKE DAMAGE` decline action no longer applies after the successful defense.

**State D2: Defender Skip/timeout/no legal Dodge.** `TAKE DAMAGE` is a *private decline-response action*, not the settled public outcome. Only after a server-confirmed real damage event show a factual public result/H4, e.g. `Zhao Yun took 1 damage.` with true affected Seat/HP. Real prevention/redirect/armor/skill outcomes must instead use their verified facts. Retain the existing readable ~2s settlement where applicable; no fabricated Dodge, second physical card or gameplay delay.

**All perspectives:** The same true card/actor/result identities must match; placement is different because physical Seats rotate for the viewer. The Local Dock highlight is **only** for the viewer with an actual pending decision. A spectator does not see or activate the defender's legal private Hand.

### 4.2 Duel

- Genuine publicly committed Duel stratagem: **Policy-B centre reveal (normally four seconds, interruptible by a new committed response)** then **first persistent card under/along the real Duel author's Seat/Dock**. During the shared Negation opportunity the genuine root Duel stays visible; uncommitted Negation intents are private. Do not display gigantic Duel/EFFECT/NEGATION panels.
- Once DUEL_EXCHANGE is authoritative, each genuinely committed Attack gets its own **Policy-B centre reveal**, which may interrupt the previous card, then **appends after that same author's previous cards** in their own anchored history. All players see actual server-proven card author and card identity, never a guessed alternation; Dodge cannot answer Duel.
- **Direction by physical seat topology (§8.1):** top-row duelist → cards vertically beneath their Hero; left-side duelist → horizontally inward to the right; right-side duelist → horizontally inward to the left; local duelist → horizontally across the battlefield immediately above the Local Dock. The spectator's screen can therefore contain **two distinct seat-attached Duel histories** simultaneously. No single global bottom Dock rail.
- **Chronology across both participants:** The actual root Duel is event #1; each subsequently confirmed Attack is #2, #3, #4, etc. Add compact globally unique order badges to the physical CardFaces so that a spectator knows whether Cao Cao or Zhen Ji answered next even when their cards are in separate columns. Never replace a previous card by its author or collapse history into latest-only +N.
- Histories are locally scrollable in their **own orientation** when many cards exceed safe battlefield space, never over hero portraits, other seats, response timer, central reveal, Hand, Guidance or Confirm/Skip. Keep proven settlement visible ~2s before orderly retirement. No second public CardFace, fabricated pending Attack, connector/graph or reopened root reveal on refresh.
### 4.3 Negation and counter-Negation

- With an open Negation opportunity but **no committed Negation**, show **only the original public action's card** in its genuine current centre-reveal or actor-parked phase and one compact public effect sentence; no provisional Negation CardFace, no private responder leak, no verbose `NEGATION RESPONSE`, `EFFECT` or `REACTION CHAIN` panel.
- **Exactly one current genuine card occupies the large central reveal slot at a time;** each newly confirmed Negation/counter-Negation may interrupt the previous centre card (§8.7), and the previous physical card parks under/alongside its **actual author's Seat** (or true local Dock), **never grouped with all responses in a central row**. The new response then reveals alone at centre before itself parking under its own Hero; the most recent public response is strongest. **Each still-visible genuine physical card gets its global within-chain ordinal (#1 root, #2 onward real responses), including repeated responses by the same author, per new §10.7.2.1 R2-D.** Older same-interaction cards remain subdued/compact and a history count may replace overflow **only when permitted without losing the ability to determine real physical card order**, and the one narration band remains stable near battlefield centre (§10.8.4 H4-B).
- Only server-proof outcome decides `effect negated`, `effect restored` or continuation. Preserve per-viewer private Local Dock's `Play Negation or Pass` (or `Pass` only) options and one shared, server-authoritative window deadline. Never infer parity of the public counter chain to change game rules.

### 4.4 Group/AOE and other public cards

- Root Barbarian Invasion, Raining Arrows, Oath etc. genuine public played card: **Policy-B centre reveal** → source's owner-side thumbnail. Subsequent *actually committed* Attack/Dodge/Negation from eligible participants reveal individually and park before each true author. Compact per-player `waiting / responded / avoided / damaged` information must be backed by real public statuses; no imaginary responses or per-target connectors.
- For a current Raining Arrows defender, use the authoritative Dodge providers; keep unrelated Hand cards visible but subdued. If no legal Dodge is available, the explicit local choice may be `TAKE DAMAGE` (per server-projected legal action), **not** a meaningless disabled Confirm. Numeric damage is never guessed.
- Equipment, Judgement, Private Draw, Harvest, theft/discard selection and Hero continuations retain their true card/public-private rules in §5. **Every server-committed public skill outcome is owned by one factual H4** (R2/R7), without a parallel generic skill-result toast or invented CardFace. A distinct compact SYSTEM notification is allowed only for a separately justified *non-outcome* event, not as a second copy of H4.

## 5. Existing UX2 functional designs that remain approved

These are **current functional requirements**, not old combat-graph tasks. They remain in force during both migration phases.

### 5.1 Hero Skills band (old §1)

- The Local Hero's Skills area next to the portrait is the **single activation entry point** for the viewer's actionable Hero skills. Fill available width: one skill uses full Skills band; two peer skills use equal width and equal height; names wrap naturally (prefer one line for Cao Cao's `TREACHERY` / `ENTOURAGE` when room permits). Touch targets at least **44×44 CSS px**; no clipped text or character-by-character wrapping.
- Skills requiring legal card/target/card-position selection launch the existing authoritative selection flow. Under independently owner-approved **§10.61** (when implemented), a reversible local precommit draft with an always-visible activator uses **the same named button again to exit**, retappable selected cards/targets, and the existing primary **Confirm**; it must not add a redundant local Cancel or `Use <Hero Skill>` CTA. An actual mandatory/standalone chooser without a reversible activator may retain the existing explicit Cancel/Skip according to its separate semantics. Until §10.61 ships, this is an approved future design, not evidence the older UI has already passed. Passives are clearly non-actionable; optional triggers become actionable only from server-projected choice.
- A *different* Hero's passive response choice offered to this viewer is a Local Dock decision, **not** an activation button for the viewer's Hero. Retain provider IDs, revisions, selection reset, privacy and all Standard roster reachability.

### 5.2 Zhuge Liang skills and private Stargazing (old §4)

- Actionable **Stargazing** is distinguishable from passive **Empty Fortress Strategem**; no duplicate generic action skill button. Private Stargazing has **three vertically ordered zones**: `TOP OF DECK`, `REVEALED CARDS`, `BOTTOM OF DECK`.
- Four initially private revealed CardFaces begin in the middle zone; Top and Bottom begin empty but remain usable drop zones. **Whole-card (not grip-only) touch-hold/pointer drag** supports assignment, moving between zones and reordering **within** a zone; visible insertion indicator, no duplicated/lost card, cancelled drag leaves prior state, normal scrolling when not dragging. **User-reported current grip-only touch limitation is explicitly targeted by §10.11 G1/G2; do not claim the existing code already meets this requirement.** On a ~390px phone show the four recognisable cards together when feasible; compact zones, contained overflow when necessary, no huge three-buttons-per-card grid.
- `Top` left→right is next draws first; `Bottom` left→right is placed after the remaining deck. Local lists `unassigned`, `top`, `bottom` each contain each card ID **exactly once**. `COMPLETE STARGAZING` is enabled only when `unassigned` is empty and authoritative `minTop/maxTop` constraints are satisfied; **all-to-Top or all-to-Bottom is legal if allowed**. Existing submission contract remains `{providerId:"private_deck_reorder",topCardIds,bottomCardIds}`. Keep server order, revision/busy guards, private-only visibility and keyboard/screen-reader movement alternative.

### 5.3 Response guidance, timer and System Menu (old §§3, 4A)

- The Local Guidance area describes the **actual next decision** (`Play Dodge or Skip`, `Play Negation or Pass/Skip` using the actual server-authorized label, `Take Damage` when the authoritative alternative applies), rather than implementation jargon like `Group Resolution`. Only real legal options are emphasized; unrelated Hand stays visible, subdued if appropriate. No guessed damage amounts.
- Response/event timers share a **compact lower-right battlefield cluster** just above Guidance: numeric timer typically ~52–68×36–44px for response or ~44–64×36–44px for event, separated from the ≥44×44px System Menu button by about 8px. Do not keep a second big timer at the top of the combat screen; preserve exact server deadline, privacy and visible-at rules.
- System Menu remains compact and contains **build SHA, Download UX trace and Exit Game**; no large diagnostics essay, preserve privacy filtering, JSON export, exit confirmation and useful short download feedback.
- When an expired or unavailable room returns to the entry screen, clear the saved room session, explain why the game ended, and keep any tab-local UX trace downloadable there; the render-error recovery screen must also retain trace export. Explicit Exit Game clears the saved session so reload does not restore the departed room.
- **Private Draw display (owner update 2026-10-30 23:01 UTC; R6-QA-03):** The actual newly received CardFace(s) remain visible **only to their authenticated owner** in the short existing private reveal, then enter that player's Hand. **Do not show the decorative `PRIVATE DRAW` / `ONLY YOU CAN SEE THESE CARDS` title/explanation box** above those cards. Maintain a private screen-reader region label (e.g. `Private drawn cards`) that is not a visible banner, retain the existing reveal duration, event timer, Hand update, inspect/accessibility behavior, privacy gating and content-driven layout without a large empty Stage. This later owner decision supersedes this section's former requirement for a visible `ONLY YOU CAN SEE THESE CARDS` label. Bumper Harvest and other event-specific timers continue to share the compact cluster; never leak card identity to another viewer.
- **Public skill outcome presentation — owner-approved R6-QA-02 (2026-10-30 23:12 UTC):** Every server-committed, publicly authorized skill result is shown through **one canonical H4/public-event narration**, whether it has a real physical CardFace or not; **no duplicate upper-right `EFFECT TRIGGERED` / `effectNotice` toast** for that same result. The one public H4 shares authoritative event identity/order, §10.45 fixed-upper measured H4 geometry, authentic fact lifetime and accessible announcement. Do not invent a public CardFace for a no-physical-card skill result, derive a gained card name from private Hand, or guess damage from HP changes/adjacent events. PAC independently shows only the next public action; Console only authorized viewer choices; genuine private draw cards remain owner-private. A separate small system/non-outcome transient notice may remain only if it has an independently justified purpose and does **not** repeat a committed H4 result. This replaces the older blanket generic skill-result-toast policy, not the separate game rules or actual timers.

### 5.4 Unified compact player Inspect (old §4B.12)

- **Exactly one** compact public-information inspector. Selecting a legal opponent or using their Inspect affordance opens/updates the **same panel**, never `PREVIEW TARGET` + separate overlapped `INSPECT`. Changing the *inspection subject* alone must not silently change the legally selected gameplay target; clicking a legal target Seat may explicitly select it. Closing Inspect preserves an existing valid target/Confirm state.
- iPhone 17 Pro Max-style black/gold **upper/middle battlefield floating panel**, not full screen: use ~88–94% of usable safe width where feasible and content-driven height, roughly 40–50% battlefield-height cap with **internal scrolling** for overflow. Do not cover opponent Seats, Hand, Guidance or Confirm/Cancel; one close × control, no `View Details` button and no automatic full detail screen.
- Show name, recognisable Hero portrait, public HP, Hand **count** (not card identities), named public skills, actual public Equipment Weapon/Armour/+1/−1 horse, and public Judgment cards or `None`. Any hidden role remains hidden. **Tap a skill to show its canonical NAME and description in the shared Details region**; tapping another skill or visible public equipment/Judgement card switches the description directly. **Retapping a skill or public-zone Details item *inside an already open Inspector* does not collapse/clear that detail** (§8.5), and cannot activate skills. This differs from retapping the **outer Hero/Card Inspector-opening affordance** for the same subject, which may close the whole Inspector per §10.7 R4-I without changing the selected gameplay target. No invented equipment or hand-back identities.
- Use actual server public data; compare equipment-rich supported 4/5/6-seat cases and long skill descriptions, ensuring no overlapping panels or disabled local controls. **Inspector must keep long descriptions scrollable; tapping public equipment or another skill directly switches its named detail in the same open Inspector, with NO nested Back; inspected Hero and any legitimate selected target remain (§8.3–§8.5).** Equipment mini-card rank/suit/art must scale together.

### 5.5 Server-authorized target-card picker — one shared component

The **actor-private target-card selector** is distinct from passive Hero Inspector, but it reuses the **single bounded nonmodal `BattlefieldChoiceStage + CardPoolPanel`** under **R5 / §10.17 / accepted §10.22**. It has genuine legal zone tabs, one selectable candidate row, meaningful selected-count, separate public Info and only server-legal Confirm/Use/Skip (no duplicate Dock action, generic Cancel/Clear or invisible hit shield). Selection/retap, minimum touch geometry, mixed zones and safe short-phone scroll obey shared R5 rather than a new picker design.

**Only unique authority details here:** Separately provided `hand:<position>` keys represent real anonymous selectable face-down backs; an offered **single `hand` zone key** represents **one grouped/random-zone choice**, never invented separate positions. Eligible genuine public Equipment/Judgement appear face-up and remain inspectable; empty/ineligible zones are omitted. Real `CurrentAction` keys, revisions and min/max control Confirm; Frost Sword uses its own actual server-authorized min/max; current committed discard implementation requires exactly two distinct eligible cards (§10.78). Do not generalize its count to Steal/Dismantle/Retaliation or change rules in UX work. Large Hands swipe inside the selector; no spectator hidden identity. Genuine effect-specific discard/acquire/Skip semantics live in §§10.10/10.46.

## 6. Visual quality and production-path acceptance

**Mandatory functional checks for CURRENT supported new matches (no fixture-only completion claims):** genuine server-backed 4/5/6-player games, with authorized host-controlled test players for identity switches. Use representative iPhone widths 390px, ~440px, and 480px when appropriate; **do not require 7/8-player iPhone visual runs, unfinished layout fixes or screenshot acceptance**. An existing larger-game server compatibility regression may still be retained independently of new-game visual review.

**New visual invariants:**
- **Zero** source-target/card-card relationship strokes, SVG arrows, old connector portals, historical route lines or per-player branch visualization.
- **Zero** old giant `EFFECT / NEGATION RESPONSE / REACTION CHAIN / Duel Exchange metadata` panels over normal gameplay.
- One centre public CardFace during each legitimate reveal; no second legacy copy. A truthful per-player thumbnail appears only when that author really played a public card.
- Real viewer-specific top Seats/Local Dock do not move. The public card for a given actor parks before that actor in **all** views; no player-to-card identity mismatch.
- Legal controls, visible response timer, touchable Hand, Inspect, skill buttons and System Menu continue functioning while cards reveal/park. No page horizontal scrolling, Seat/Guidance/Dock overlap or clipped card.
- Skip and timeout never generate fictitious Dodge or unsupported damage. Real blocked/Damage outcome visible near actual target long enough to read.
- Fast response/poll/refresh/test-view switch/reduced motion cannot repeat or leak a historical reveal. Public presentation never reveals hidden Hand identities or a private responder.

**Evidence grading:** Rule/API pass → public projection pass → browser fixture pass → real authenticated browser/game path pass → deployed device screenshot → **user visual acceptance**. CI or a mounted DOM element alone never proves the final UX. Capture actual visible screenshot and IDs/build SHA; report unsupported proof/fallback honestly.

**CI policy:** normal required push-to-CI-to-Cloudflare deployment/smoke target **≤6:00 wall clock**. Retain small meaningful build/typecheck, lint, rules/API/privacy and startup smoke; do not add large unapproved visual matrices to mandatory push CI. Optional **4/5/6 supported-game** visual cases stay focused and within the Agent's total local UI-test budget; **no 7/8 phone-test queue**. Preserve existing engine-level legacy game compatibility tests and do not suppress real safety errors.

## 7. Historical baseline migration — ARCHIVE ONLY (DO NOT EXECUTE)

**Completed previous work:** UX2-NO-GRAPH-REMOVAL-01 removed the old visual relation/graph UI and introduced a truthful, playable static public card view. UX2-CENTRE-REVEAL-SEAT-CARDS-02 then introduced proof-backed centre CardFace reveal and true actor-seat parking, beginning with Attack/Dodge. Both are historic migrations; **do not rerun, restore the old UI, or use old migration acceptance timing**.

**Current visual authority:** §§1–6, **§8.7 Policy B** and family-specific accepted sections. The initial migration's old static presenter, approximate 500–800ms read, 250–450ms travel, historic Step D sequencing and graph-specific source observations are **superseded**, not future work. Previous detailed implementation and CI history is available through Git history, not active task instructions.

**Next work:** Follow the current execution index at the top. Coding Agent owns `HANDOVER.md`; pending Reviewer acceptance does not block another authorized UX2 task.

## 8. Accepted Phase 1 — real public cards, per-author history, compact Info and Policy B

### 8.1 Duel and physically played Attack chronological history — USER-ACCEPTED design baseline

Every actually committed Duel/Attack response/card retains **its own real printed card identity, actor, event order, original root and target**, including multiple attacks/counters by the same player. Preserve all genuine public cards simultaneously as **per-actual-Seat/Dock author-attached history**, not an oversized central causality chain. **TOP Seats:** compact genuine thumbnails stack vertically under portrait. **LEFT/RIGHT Seats:** row fans horizontally **inward** toward battlefield. **Local Hero Dock:** history goes horizontally above Dock. Global ordered labels on proven physical events are readable (including later genuine Negation ordinals, reusable R3 / §10.7 R2-D). Newly committed events can interrupt earlier central reveal without destroying earlier histories, even if Inspector opens, TEST SWITCH changes viewer or the game reconnects. No fake printed Duel for skill-generated Lust, and no physical card reattributed from original actor because of turn or layout.

### 8.2 Normal battlefield presentation — no large permanent Deck/Discard piles

Do not leave a physical DECK or DISCARD CardFace pile permanently in the central battle. Draw/discard/equipment placement follows true server zones, public disclosure and private recipient boundaries. An unrelated skill effect cannot create a fake played card.

### 8.3–8.5 Real Hero/Card Inspector, Equipment miniature and names — USER-ACCEPTED

One compact, safe nonmodal real **selected Hero Inspector** shows genuine portrait, all active named skills with their complete actual text, and owned publicly proven Equipment/Judgement. Public equipped miniatures use recognizable art, suit/rank and exact known identity without illegible printed miniature-card words; Info switches detail within the current panel, no nested modal/Back chain or lost Hero focus. Never expose owner-private Hand, role or hidden response options. The later **H2-G3 F2-R3 owner-accepted unified responsive Inspector** overrides older placement illustrations for its covered 4/6/8 player phone modes: actual Hero portrait LEFT, identity/HP/Hand to its RIGHT, bottom Judgement2 LEFT and Equipment4 RIGHT; 6 genuine mini slots have equal size. BCP-2 and R4-F are separate accepted Card/Hand Details behaviors.

### 8.7 Policy B Phase-1 delivery — historical technical status, not an independent owner PASS

Original Phase-1 Policy-B implementation was **technically delivered and administratively age-archived**, not automatically user phone-accepted. **§3 now solely defines the current shared REVEAL→MOVE→PARK→RETIRE timing, legitimate interruption and reduced motion**; R3 governs real card identity/author parking and accepted §10.41 governs newer independent committed play after prior final facts. Maintain default ~4s full public reveal and ~380ms ordinary author-park where legitimately applicable, without replay, private information leak or action blocking. Historical Phase-1 execution details remain in [immutable Git history](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/deb1ad92e21f2c04aaffbfeb64657e415c2764eb/docs/UX2-refine.md); there is no second competing Policy B design.

## 9. Shared-window Negation Plan A — established server rule, private intents and true public response

**Current authoritative game rule (preserve without redesign):** When a card opens a Negation response opportunity, the server runs a shared **maximum 8-second** window over eligible living seats. Each player may privately submit authorized Negation or **Pass**; selecting/locking that intent is not public. **Early finish is allowed ONLY after every living seat has explicitly submitted** (including explicit Pass, even if no Negation in Hand). A silent/disconnected participant does **not** get silently auto-Passed to force early settlement; wait until the true server deadline. The server's deterministic canonical seat-priority choice, winning response, legitimate counters and overall result are the sole authority. The deadline is immutable for that window and safe against stale/repeated/racing POSTs; related Negation/counter windows follow real server rules, not a global client timer for all other response kinds.

**Privacy and options:** `CurrentAction` exposes only each real viewer's legal Negation/Pass, private providers, submitted lock/revision and safe private guidance. An unauthorized viewer can see **only generic public PAC `Waiting for Negation responses`**, never who has a card, submitted, Passed, won, count/seat priority, exact unplayed Hand or secret role. `HANDOVER`, UI animation, timer at 0 and private response presence do not establish a public winner. Viewer switching/reconnect must not copy another participant's legal options or reveal private submission. Invalid/stale POST fails the existing server validator.

**Committed PUBLIC Negation:** Once a real winning played physical card is server committed, publish exact genuine card, author, root event, response ordinal, counter target and proven ACTIVE/BLOCKED/restored/FINAL effect. One centre Policy-B reveal then one actual author Seat/Dock attachment; distinct confirmed counters attach their separate genuine physical cards. The original played root preserves identity; if final cancelled, no fabricated target pick/Steal transfer/Duel damage; when effect is restored/confirmed, proceed only with actual legal subsequent steps. Public H4 describes only committed cancel/restore/result, PAC only the publicly possible next step, Local Console only viewer-private action (reusable R2 / newest §10.25).

**Owner acceptance:** Plan A's private all-seat submission and genuine committed counter/FINAL/Harvest response display were previously accepted; the owner **explicitly confirmed the shared Negation and complete Dismantle outcome USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED on 2026-10-31 21:13 UTC**. Continue to enforce actual game-specific effects and public/private proof under R1–R7 and §10.25 as **regression standards**, with **no old N0–N3/R6 implementation or review queue**.

Do not reinstate UX3 Plan B: immediate public CardFace on individual's Confirm is a distinct unapproved rules/privacy/fairness change (§11). Earlier 2026-10-14 Gate A/B/C and N0–N3 code/tests/CI/Worker logs and stale `NEXT TASK` instructions are available via Git history only; no fresh implementation is authorized by this section.

## 10. PHASE 3 — Other card families: controlled UX rollout

### 10.1–10.4 Card-family UX principles — accepted common grammar, not a historical task queue

The old phases, multi-card audit queue, dated `R3→R4→H1` implementation orders, `NO CHANGE` source reviews and per-family Agent checklists are **ARCHIVED** at [the immutable pre-acceptance design](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/ce13534e18abca06916b38711f3ff35a1522540d/docs/UX2-refine.md); **they do not authorize work**. A new feature should start with the **R0–R8 shared architecture**, enforce actual game-specific legality and add only a design difference justified by real evidence. Table A is the only current implementation permission.

| Family | Durable difference / design reference |
| --- | --- |
| **Negation and linked counters** | Private 8s-maximum Plan A (§9), server-confirmed winning/counter effects, real centre-to-author CardFace/history and fact-only H4/PAC; **owner accepted 2026-10-31** (§10.25). |
| **Steal / Dismantle / Borrowed Sword / Draw Two** | One genuine public root and only legal actor-private choice, concealed opponent Hand, real card zones and proper gain/discard/forced-Attack or weapon outcome; Steal/Dismantle and Negation **accepted**, other cards use their original specific server rules (§10.10). |
| **AOE/group/Harvest and recovery** | One real group root, actual per-recipient response/settlement and one current Harvest recipient's public pool turn; never treat its entire pool as blocked by a single Negation (§10.17/§10.25). |
| **Delayed Judgement** | True Overindulgence/Lightning/Eight Trigrams card attachment, later legally revealed Judgement and confirmed result; do not simulate a prior future decision or permanent DECK/DISCARD pile. |
| **Equipment and Hero skills** | Genuine physical cost/played-as material, true activation and material owners, original printed art, no fake transformed card; one reusable frame or fact-only event according to actual disclosure (§R4, §10.9). |
| **Attack / Dodge / Duel** | Preserve complete per-actor genuine history and legal response interruption (§8, §R3); no duplicated narration or public private intent. |

**General invariants:** Server legality and deadlines, card conservation, actor/target identity, secrecy and fail-closed public proof are not presentation preferences. Never reintroduce causal graphs, a parallel old public-card presenter, duplicate same-event toasts or a permanent centre gallery. Protect local Hand and decision controls while animating. New coding work requires newly approved scope, targeted privacy/API and visual regression, required CI/deployment, and the **five-minute total local UI/browser budget per bounded task**. Green CI alone is not an owner phone PASS.

### 10.5 Da Qiao Deflection (琉璃) — E1/E2 and §10.5.3 USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED (2026-10-14)

**Accepted UX:** Original server-committed Attack retains its true author, card/root and original target; Da Qiao's uncommitted cost/redirect remain PRIVATE. After confirmation, show a compact central **DEFLECTION / 琉璃 label ABOVE the authentic discarded cost CardFace**, with one accurate proven target-change explanation and **no duplicate upper-right EFFECT TRIGGERED notice**. Move the SAME paid cost/card+skill label to **Da Qiao's genuine Seat/Dock**. Show the changed Attack target from public server proof; replacement victim responds through their legal private Dodge window. Any real Dodge can interrupt the centre display and parks by its own true actor. Never replay the original Attack as a new card, invent a Dodge or change server rules. Preserve Hand/Equipment cost, remaining branches in multi-target Attack, equipment-loss triggers, privacy and no causal lines.

### 10.6 Original mobile refinements — now part of the common design

The old eight separate repair tickets are **not independent tasks or a second standard**. Their effective decisions are now owned by: **R2** for `TAKE DAMAGE` vs proven damage and deduplicated event copy; **R3** for inspectable true public parked CardFaces and one H4 card journey; **R6** for authentic compact Card Details, accessible selected-Hand **in-card DETAILS**, genuine readable PLAYER/hero identity and separate inspection vs Play; **R4/R7 and §§10.12/10.14** for publicly proven Diao Chan Lust/Beauty. Preserve these final behaviors; do not restore old screenshots, separate Info strips or the U1–U8 Agent queue.

### 10.7 R1–R4 — accepted foundations and administratively archived later refinements

**USER-ACCEPTED original baselines:** R1 legal genuinely committed Dodge promptly interrupts previous Attack centre reveal without losing original author PARKED history; R2-A/B/C genuine root/Negation/counter centre-to-true-author CardFace identity; R3 keeps `TAKE DAMAGE` private decline-action separate from a verified real public damage consequence; R4-F genuine selected Hand's **in-card DETAILS** opens compact nonmodal Inspector without playing it.

#### 10.7.2.1 R2-D — cross-actor global response ordinals (TECHNICALLY DELIVERED / AGE-ARCHIVED, NOT PHONE ACCEPTED)
Each true committed root **#1**, first Negation **#2**, proven counter **#3**, then **#4/#5…** within the same root interaction; next independent root restarts #1. Numeral is on the real physical CardFace even if two counter cards are parked under one author. High-contrast upper-right badge ~12–14px on ~48×72 thumbnail must not obscure suit/rank or adjacent cards. Only server-proven root/response ordinal/event/author/card linkage counts; NEVER number private intents, Pass, skipped seats, losing bids, or derive order from DOM. Viewer switches keep public sequence.

#### 10.7.4.2 R4-G — own-Hand inspection without action (TECHNICALLY DELIVERED / AGE-ARCHIVED)
The owner may inspect any genuinely `myHand` card outside active Play; `inspectOnlyHandCardId`-equivalent remains separate from ACTION-selected IDs, target IDs, provider, payload and Confirm. Server-authorized off-turn Dodge/Negation, Play, discard, skill-cost selection still work; disabled action cards may be inspected without POST.

#### 10.7.4.3 R4-H — circular `i` INSIDE raised Hand CardFace (TECHNICALLY DELIVERED / AGE-ARCHIVED)
High-contrast recognizable circular Info, 44px-class independent touch hit where possible, no interception of adjacent Hand or accidental Play. The genuine compact Card Details is the same R4-F inspector.

#### 10.7.4.4 R4-I — Equipment Info/tap behavior (TECHNICALLY DELIVERED / AGE-ARCHIVED)
Remove redundant separate `i` from already inspectable public Equipment miniatures. Retapping the **same Hero/Card's outer Inspector-opening control** may close the whole Inspector; tapping a different outer subject switches inspected Hero/Card without touching a gameplay target. **An internal detail item retap keeps its details displayed**, per §5.4/§8.5. No nested modal, duplicate Info, accidental target or gameplay action.

The old R2/R4 implementation instructions and screenshots are superseded; age archive is **NOT** user visual PASS. Latest reusable §R3/§R6 and §10.25 own shared card and H4 rules.

### 10.8 Own Hero / opponent Seat and stable public announcements — active final display standard

**H1-F USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED (2026-10-16):** Local own-Hero Details is a compact battlefield-bound nonmodal, genuine large illustrated portrait, canonical Hero name and **all full real named skill descriptions**. No duplicated `HERO DETAILS` headline, faction or HP text in the panel (HP remains in own permanent Dock); safe close and gameplay taps.

**H2-F / H2-G1-F / H2-G2 USER-ACCEPTED:** Genuine opponent Hero portrait is not covered by multiple redundant name bands; overlay the true **PLAYER name** on a narrow translucent readable lower portrait scrim, not the incorrect Hero-name lettering from old mockup. Keep actual hero identity, current public HP/equipment and meaningful target-vs-Inspector touch separation; tap legal portrait target only when targeting, name/Info inspects without selecting. Local own Hero has actual art and **viewer-private role + numeric HP footer**, never sent to another viewer's public projection. The earlier roughly 11px Hero/9px player-label H2-F typography pass is historic; the **newer H2-G final composition** overrides its old placement.

##### Accepted responsive Hero Seat and Inspector — current 4/5/6-player contract

**Current approved design:** Reuse **R6** for genuine viewer-relative clockwise Seat identity, compact TOP4 (five-player) / TOP5 (six-player) layout, consistent reference-width outer frames rather than enlarging TOP4 to occupy extra space, equal distributed inter-card gaps, safe symmetric margins, full-width actual Hero portrait image, readable PLAYER identity and HP, equipped slot visibility, valid touch/Inspector/SWITCH and exact public CardFace-author anchors. Preserve the accepted responsive unified Inspector: genuine selected Hero portrait LEFT; name/HP and PLAYER name/Hand RIGHT; bottom Judgement LEFT (2) and Equipment RIGHT (4), all six public mini-slots equally sized, with scroll/inspection for additional genuine Judgement. Keep all hidden roles/Hands private, prevent control collision and use actual measured DOMRect geometry.

**Historical larger-room status (not current work):** Eight-player SIDE Hero frame had been explicitly phone-accepted 2026-10-31 19:12 UTC; seven-player SIDE had no explicit PASS. Following owner decision 2026-10-31 23:04, **all pending or unfinished 7/8-player phone visual work and corner-author thumbnail spacing redesign are REMOVED FROM CURRENT SCOPE**, not a deferred review and not a fabricated acceptance. Prior exact TOP/SIDE layout measurements and screenshots are retrievable from [immutable history](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/b6b8e9b852ca115cf36d92760654d2ce33c6254a/docs/UX2-refine.md). The current six-player room gate and existing already-started legacy-game server compatibility remain untouched.

**H3 public text USER-ACCEPTED:** one short readable factual normal-width sans-serif announcement (roughly **14–16px mobile** where safe), actual **PLAYER/actor, card/skill and proven result/target**. Examples: `Player A played Attack on B.`, `B played Dodge.`; do not invent card, HP, damage or duplicate long result. During current real central REVEAL position it above the authentic physical CardFace.

**H4-A/B/C accepted, now one authoritative definition:** Keep one readable factual public H4 (not waiting/phase copy or duplicated generic toast), separate current-step PAC and private viewer Console. **R2** owns public/private responsibilities, typography and the measured PAC/Console seam; **§10.25.2** owns exact Negation/Harvest English copy; **§10.45** owns all latest shared vertical upper-H4 / stable CardFace / chooser placement. Earlier stage-centred H4 positioning is not a second accepted contract. Retain genuine full names and growing line height, horizontally safe x, lawful fact lifetime and valid touch targets.

**Historical H1–H4 screenshots, code repair steps and previously accepted eight-player SIDE evidence remain in Git.** All **7/8-player unfinished UI or phone-review tasks are out of scope**, not deferred; current accepted TOP/Inspector/H4 principles continue to govern supported 4/5/6.

### 10.9 W1–W6 — one proof-fed real-card-backed Hero/Equipment Frame (TECHNICALLY DELIVERED / AGE-ARCHIVED, NOT OWNER-PHONE ACCEPTED)

**Reusable architecture:** `docs/UX2_PUBLIC_CARD_PROOF_INVENTORY.md` and repository rules classify **30 Standard Heroes / 46 skills / 9 Weapons / 2 Armors / 2 Mounts**. W1 public proof; W2 shared renderer; W3 COST; W4 conversion/Judgement; W5 Equipment EFFECT; W6 integration. One compact framed true skill/equipment name **ABOVE actual publicly provable physical CardFace(s)**; one card centre, two side-by-side, 3+ in accessible compact row/grid; no duplicate same-event toast. Server-confirmed provenance binds **activation owner, material owner, exact physical card, root/event/target, effect**. The target who really paid/discarded a public card is its material owner even if another Hero triggered it. Secret/random Hand remains anonymous; non-card-backed effect uses text-only H4. An AS conversion retains ORIGINAL art/rank/suit with verified **AS ATTACK / AS DODGE / AS PEACH / AS OVERINDULGENCE**, not a fake printed transformed card. Follow Policy-B real centre-to-author/material-owner park, no legal-action blocking.

**W3 COST examples:** Hua Tuo **Prodigal Healer** genuine paid recovery, Lady Gan **Divine Wisdom** three exact paid Hand cards plus true recovery, Yue Jin **Dauntless** Basic COST/target continuation, **Betrothment** two genuine paid cards, **Equilibrium** true Hand/Equipment COST with PRIVATE replacement draw, and **LUST**. Prior COST event must retire its H4 when a later genuine public event owns the stage; the specific W3-F2-R1 flicker was **USER-ACCEPTED 2026-10-28**, not entire W3 scope.

**W4 conversions/Judgement:** Hua Tuo **First Aid AS PEACH**, Guan Yu **God of War** red **AS ATTACK**, Zhao Yun **Braveheart** Dodge AS ATTACK / Attack AS DODGE, Empress Dowager black AS DODGE, Da Qiao **Captivating AS OVERINDULGENCE** in real delayed Judgement. Keep actual Guicai replacement Judgement and final zone, **Staunch­ness two real paid cards only on actual discard**, true original source/card and legal server response.

**W5 Equipment:** **Rock Cleaving Axe** two true cost cards; **Kirin Bow** committed target-owned Mount discard; **Yin-Yang Swords** actual public discard versus private attacker draw; **Frost Sword** exactly two real target Hand/Equipment discards and prevented damage; **Green Dragon Blade** true follow-up Attack linked to original dodged Attack; **Nio Shield** black Attack immunity, Eight Trigrams actual Judgement response. Do not pre-display, forge or remove Equipment before the real authoritative zone movement.

**Regression design:** true original card/actor/payer/recipient on all authenticated viewers, actual physical conservation, known public vs hidden Hand, correct response continuation and legal `CurrentAction`, safe current 4/5/6-player phone geometry. Reuse **R1–R4**, not one-off Hero-name selectors. Old per-SHA coding, large UI matrices and deployment histories are not current tasks; owner age closure records technical delivery, **NOT** a blanket phone acceptance.

### 10.10 Steal / Burning Bridges — one shared target-card interaction, no extra public result card

**Final design:** The ordinary effective Steal (顺手牵羊) gains a single real selected target card, while effective Burning Bridges / Dismantle (过河拆桥) discards one; the **server** owns legality, target zone, actual original card/owner and successful settlement. The public root and committed Negations are genuine one-card journeys under **R3**, public H4/PAC follow **R2**, and only the actor's actual `CurrentAction` shows the **R5 private target-card picker**. Known public Equipment/Judgement identities stay public; an unrevealed opposing Hand remains anonymous. The recipient sees a genuine newly obtained concealed card only via **R7**; everyone else sees at most a proven anonymous gain/zone count. No premature transfer or `took` while final Negation has cancelled the effect.

**Accepted shared presentation (2026-10-31 14:49 UTC):** Do **NOT** show a second centre result CardFace, striped anonymous public card back, target-to-Hand/Discard transport, duplicate settlement caption or result popup — **even when the affected card's identity was already publicly known**. Keep the authentic original played card and counter CardFaces in their actual authors' Seat/Dock histories, along with real Equipment/Judgement/Discard state. The public settlement is **one fact-only H4** and the next public activity is **one stable lower PAC**. The actor's chosen target zone and private options are never projected to spectators before commitment. A successful publicly proven Dismantle may name the discarded face only AFTER it is public, e.g. `JD used Burning Bridges on Test Player 2, discarding [♦A Card Name] from their Equipment.` — substitute proven actor, player, exact suit/rank/name/zone; otherwise use a generic truthful result. H4 retires on actual later public event or return to ordinary Play, not on a removed result-card geometry timer.

**Owner-approved completion:** The actor-private Steal/Dismantle chooser and basic effect were accepted 2026-10-27; the shared public presentation passed 2026-10-31 14:49. **The owner additionally confirms the full Negation and Dismantle flows USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED at 21:13 UTC on 2026-10-31.** That includes Dismantle root → actual committed Negation cancellation → linked genuine counter restoration → authorized card choice → one true discard, while preserving privacy and existing R2/H4/PAC behavior. **No remaining task or review is attached to this accepted design.** Future changes must preserve this as a regression contract; only a new observed failure can justify a new distinct task.

#### 10.24.1 ROOM-6P-GATE-01 — USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED (2026-10-31 20:14 UTC)
