Seven/eight/nine/ten-player new-game selections or admissions are unavailable. **Owner explicitly passed this delivered scope** on 2026-10-31 20:14 UTC and **reaffirmed PASS on 2026-11-01 00:01 UTC**. No repeat build or implementation is requested. Code [`ac7fd80f`](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/commit/ac7fd80f01b306ca15c5985f8b1f60a0e24f4cda); exact-SHA [Actions 41236301619](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/actions/runs/41236301619) succeeded; Worker `9d2377ed-0123-4ef6-8787-6f65d665618a`.

A **single current authoritative maximum of six** governs new room capacity, legitimate independent joins, host-controlled Test Player additions, Test mode enablement and start transitions for all **pre-start** rooms, regardless of stale client or persisted legacy maximum. UI is a truthful representation of server capacity, never the only enforcement. Preserve at least four participants to start, server authentication, race-safe seat admission and card/role/hero authority.

Earlier 7/8 layout diagrams and the accepted old 8-player SIDE frame stay retrievable in immutable Git history, **not in a pending/rework queue**.

**Current regression contract:** New lobby shows **4–6** truthfully, offers at most six seats, starts with four/five/six and denies seventh direct or concurrent API/Test-Player admission. Oversized legacy lobby still shows its full roster but cannot start over cap; **already-started legacy games continue reliably and privately**. Keep existing automated larger-game engine/compatibility checks where they already exist, but **do not create or maintain 7/8-player pending mobile visual tests/tasks**. The old `NEG-COPY-1`, `R6-ARCH` and `ST-DM-NEG-UX-01` codes remain only in historical Git revisions. This is one canonical reusable design, not a live task or phone-review backlog.

#### 10.25.1 Committed Negation/counter grammar and event authority

- **Proof and secrecy:** The shared server-owned Negation Plan A (§9) sets max **8-second** shared window and early finish only when every living player explicitly submits; all individual pending Negation/Pass choices stay PRIVATE. Only the server's genuinely committed winning/counter physical cards may create public CardFaces, H4 facts and global committed-response ordinals. A private lock, local `CurrentAction`, timer 0, stale timeline, DOM geometry, or parity guess is never evidence of a public card or effect. Every cross-view event must match actual interaction/root/checkpoint/revision/event ID, author, target, played card and confirmed CANCELLED/RESTORED/FINAL direction. **Fail closed** on uncertain proof, no invented opponent Hand or fake result.
- **Three surfaces and one physical lifecycle:** **R2** controls fact-only H4, proven-next PAC, private Console and phone geometry; **R3** controls exactly one genuine public CardFace centre → **real author Seat/Dock**, interruption on newer committed response, viewer rebase and separate presentation clocks. At a real newer public event or response/settlement handoff, retire obsolete root/counter H4; when no new committed public fact exists, PAC alone may show the waiting/next actor. Never claim an additional legal counter after true FINAL.
- **Card effect remains specific:** A genuinely committed Negation blocks **the proven root effect on the true affected recipient**. A linked genuine counter restores that effect only if the server settles it that way. No actor-only selection, transfer/discard, damage, future counter or card purchase is invented when cancelled. The root CardFace remains the original player's genuine card, not replaced by an imagined counter-effect physical card. Harvest blocks/restores **the current authorized recipient's single pick**, NOT the whole public pool/all recipients; pool stays read-only during response. Other delayed, group and forced-Attack effects follow their own server rules and evidence.
- **Private gained Hand:** R7 governs one genuine recipient-only new-card display, not public acquired-card face or `PRIVATE DRAW` title. Public copy can say `Test Player 4 used Treachery and obtained the card that caused the damage.` when proven, but never identify an unrevealed acquired card as `Duel` or expose its physical suit/rank.

#### 10.25.2 Approved H4/PAC words by effect (specific examples, not separate positioning designs)

The entire **H4/PAC/Console positioning, width, clock precedence, privacy and announcement lifecycle** is defined ONCE in **R2**. These example English wordings are **normative semantic templates**; substitute proven actual PLAYER names, root card, recipient and true effect.

| Proven event/stage | H4 = committed fact **only** | PAC = next public possibility **only while open** |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Duel root played | `King played Duel on Test Player 4.` | `Waiting for counter responses` / `Negation can cancel the Duel effect on Test Player 4.` |
| First committed Negation blocks Duel | `Test Player 3's Negation cancels King's Duel effect on Test Player 4.` | `Another Negation can restore the Duel effect on Test Player 4.` |
| Genuine linked counter restores Duel | `Test Player 2 counters Test Player 3's Negation. The Duel effect on Test Player 4 is restored.` | `Another Negation can cancel the Duel effect on Test Player 4.` |
| Committed later linked counter | State proven actor, prior counter and exact CANCELLED/RESTORED outcome only | Only a further real legal public counter; **none after FINAL** |
| `DUEL_EXCHANGE` is proven | Retire older root/Negation sentence; show only a NEW committed genuine Duel Attack or later real damage/prevention | `Waiting for [proven responder] to play Attack or take damage` / `Duel Exchange` |
| Harvest root / recipient's publicly known turn | `[actor] played Bumper Harvest.` (root), then newly proven result only | `Waiting for [recipient] to pick a card` / `Negation can prevent [recipient] from picking.` when proven |
| Committed Negation on one Harvest recipient | `[actor]'s Negation blocks [recipient]'s pick.` | `Another Negation can restore [recipient]'s pick.` while counter is still allowed |
| Genuine Harvest counter restores recipient pick | `[actor] counters [prior actor]'s Negation. [recipient]'s pick is restored.` | Next truthful possible response, or legal recipient choice only after server final closure |

Never invent a Harvest pick or damage just because a response window ended. Skill outcomes with no public physical material use a **text-only H4** as in R7. For Steal/Dismantle accepted public wording and NO second result CardFace, use **§10.10**, not a second renderer or copy generator here.

#### 10.25.3 Source-specific outcomes — reusable regression contracts, no old review queue

**USER-ACCEPTED:** For **Dismantle (过河拆桥)**, genuine public root → committed Negation **cancels the target's discard** → server-committed counter **restores** → authorized actor-only Hand/Equipment/Judgement choice → exactly **one real Discard** publicly reported only to the extent authorized. Do not display a second public result CardFace, private Hand face or fake animation. All H4/PAC/Console and genuine author-seat timing rules stay as in **R1–R7** and accepted §10.10. **Steal** has the same source-valid cancellation/restoration grammar but a **private recipient-only card gain**, never a public stolen Hand CardFace.

**Reusable differences for other card mechanics (NOT an inferred list of new tasks or separately claimed iPhone tests):** Borrowed Sword cancellation forbids a forced Attack/Weapon transfer, restoration enables only proven legal victim and weapon-holder outcomes; Overindulgence/Lightning separate initial delayed attachment from later actual Judgement; group/AOE and Harvest use target/recipient-specific Negation effects (Harvest blocks **only its current pick**, not the entire shared pool), keeping public pool read-only while responses are pending. In every case a linked server event determines actual cancellation, restoration and final effect; no guessed outcome or hidden intent is made public.

**Status:** Shared Negation and Dismantle are **USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED**. No NEG-COPY or Dismantle Coding Agent or owner-phone-review task remains. ### 10.48 HARVEST-H4-PAC-VISIBILITY-COPY-01 — Bumper Harvest public H4/PAC, chooser and viewer continuity (**USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED 2026-11-14 19:02 UTC**)

**Owner verdict and accepted boundary:** Owner explicitly confirmed “验收通过” at 2026-11-14 19:02 UTC after the final iPhone PAC correction. This acceptance closes the ORIGINAL §10.48 and all formerly §10.49 Harvest follow-up defects merged into it, including first-to-second/later-recipient PAC visibility. The preceding code was deployed at SHA 5a07913e13ebbae9c7809de49a3f120ea1bfa4f2; Actions 43628142658 passed after a failed-job retry, and the Worker was deployed. Technical evidence is not the reason for acceptance: **the owner's phone PASS is**. This verdict does NOT close independent §10.45 or make a claim about unreviewed seat counts/game modes.

**Retained current UX2 contract — mandatory for future changes and regressions:**

1. **H4, PAC and private Console have separate responsibilities.** H4 tells the most recent PROVEN, SERVER-COMMITTED public fact. PAC tells every viewer the CURRENT proven public recipient/next step. Only the authenticated viewer's CurrentAction authorizes private Negation/Pass or choose_harvest actions and Console/Hand information. The root H4 is “[source] played Bumper Harvest.”; during an actual open Negation window, PAC may say “Negation can prevent [recipient] from picking.” A committed cancellation H4 says “[actor]'s Negation blocks [recipient]'s pick.”, and an actual eligible linked counter PAC says “Another Negation can restore [recipient]'s pick.” A committed restoration H4 says “[actor] counters [prior actor]'s Negation. [recipient]'s pick is restored.”, with further counter PAC only while legally open. In genuine sequential choice, PAC says “Waiting for [recipient] to pick a card” (or equally accurate proven current action). Never substitute generic whole-card effect messages, repeat a stale root H4 after a newer event, advertise an unavailable counter, or publicly infer private intent.
2. **Committed pick facts and real selected cards.** Each confirmed pick is represented by exactly one authenticated public event/projection associating the real Harvest root/interaction/rootFrame, actor, the specific already revealed cardId and genuine kind/rank/suit, event order/checkpoint and successful authoritative transfer. Do not synthesize public pick facts from client selection, timer expiry, history string or hidden Hand. Latest H4 after a pick says “[actor] took [rank][suit] [card] from Bumper Harvest.” until a NEWER genuinely committed public event supersedes it. The picked card remains visibly in its ORIGINAL pool slot, labelled “Taken by [actor]”, inspectable but nonselectable; conserve the one real physical card with no duplicate acquisition or hidden Hand disclosure.
3. **One stable central Harvest pool and genuine controls.** Show the real public pool at normal readable size, including public Info/scroll while read-only during Negation; expose one Confirm and actual selectable cards only to the server-authorized chooser. Its private Console says “Choose 1 card from Bumper Harvest, then tap Confirm choice.”, not meaningless “Sequential Choice”, and has no second Confirm. Across the same root and unchanged viewport/Seat/real obstacle geometry, the pool top drifts no more than 2 CSS px when the actor footer or read-only phase changes; reserve the real max chooser/Confirm footprint and expand downwards. A true viewport/Inspector change may cause a newly measured safe reflow. One proven ORIGINAL root CardFace may be covered by its OWN same-root public pool (not by unrelated pools); H4/PAC, actual response/counter cards, timer, Seats, Inspector, Console and genuine actionable pool controls remain clear and accessible. No arbitrary shrinking, gutter relocation or unconditional overlay exception.
4. **Public PAC must persist across all real recipients.** Root first-window PAC, the next recipient's Negation PAC, the same recipient's choice PAC and later recipients' PAC all remain ACTUALLY visible when the server proves their public stage; DOM presence while visibility is hidden does not satisfy the contract. H4's latest *fact* eventId is distinct from the current original root/Negation-response *physical Journey* eventId. The public renderer may link them using its validated non-positional stagePrimaryEventId / data-public-card-stage-primary-event-id, while preserving the true latest H4 fact ID and copyKey. Keep H4 stage-fixed during a public pool rather than dragging it to an author's parked miniature. Shared stage identity must match the same root, interaction, current physical primary event and viewer, with exactly one owned H4; never reuse stale checkpoint/viewer/layout generations or invent a fake public CardFace. The existing PAC layout resolves a valid current shared generation and recovers from temporary pending measurement without remaining indefinitely hidden. Protect all genuine obstacles; PAC lower edge stays within 2 CSS px of measured private Console guidance and is otherwise collision-free. No forced visibility, second PAC or unsafe geometry bypass.
5. **One genuine source-attached root miniature and viewer-safe switching.** After original Bumper Harvest root reveal/move, park the authentic approximately 32×48-class history card immediately below the real source player's Seat (the Sima Yi source Seat in the reviewed view), or the true source Dock in that player's own perspective. Genuine committed Negation responder minis remain attached to their true authors and correct ordinal history. TEST SWITCH must rebase authentic history geometry to the new viewer without replaying reveal, flashing prior-viewer placement, duplicating the card or permanently hiding valid history. Public H4, PAC, pool and committed pick remain globally coherent; each viewer sees ONLY their own legal response/locked submission state, own Hand and private Console. Switching/polling never extends or restarts the server's genuine response deadline.

**Retained regression/acceptance guard:** Preserve correct first→second→third Harvest recipient H4/PAC/chooser continuity, current actor action permissions, actual PAC computed visibility, pool top and PAC/Console measured clearance, source-seat mini rebase and the correct publicly committed pick; reject stale or mismatched actor/root/interaction/viewer/event proofs and hidden intent leakage. Use focused existing tests/CI appropriate to a future change and request a fresh owner phone review for newly changed visible UX; do not require a Coding Agent to replay an entire game to rediscover a defect already demonstrated in an owner video. No visibility:visible CSS override, simulated server event, protected-target overlap, Negation Plan A rule change or second public renderer is permitted.

**History:** Old work plans, diagnostic loops, failed videos and prior §10.49 text remain at [the immutable pre-closure revision](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/559dec0897db4cbda16401422a6f84f768c00522/docs/UX2-refine.md). They are not active task instructions. §10.48 is CLOSED; §10.45 was independently USER-ACCEPTED/CLOSED on 2026-11-15 00:29 UTC.

### 10.49 CAO-CAO-ENTOURAGE-SIMULTANEOUS-WEI-DODGE-01 — unified 8-second Shared Response Window (**USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED 2026-11-15 21:34 UTC**)

**Explicit owner iPhone acceptance and boundary:** On 2026-11-15 at **21:34 UTC** the owner confirmed ALL THREE real-iPhone tests passed: **(1) Liu Bei 激将/Influencing** resolves correctly after the common 8-second Attack-response countdown; **(2) Huang Gai 濒死/Peach** accepts a real Peach, completes the rescue and continues; **(3) Cao Cao 护驾/Entourage** shared Dodge and its final outcome also pass. This is an **OWNER USER-ACCEPTED / CLOSED** verdict for the ORIGINAL unified §10.49, not a CI-derived PASS and not a new F1 task. Previous Liu Bei expiry and Dying Peach Confirm videos were captured on **pre-fix** build `fc3496a0` and are historical, no longer OPEN/P0 for the accepted scope. This PASS does **not** claim every conversion, saved-room case, edge HP, or viewport was separately tested on iPhone; the mandatory regression contracts below stay effective. §10.49 is distinct from the older Harvest §10.49 merged into §10.48. Historical sequential §10.50 was previously accepted but superseded for NEW Entourage activations by this unified design.

**Technical provenance, independent of human verdict:** Code/test correction `a88becfd2706413c09873c75313a1cc49a73070d` shipped in `991fe948987e5914e2275aea3207366c57f24b46`; [exact-SHA Actions 43750729642](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/actions/runs/43750729642) passed API, lint/fast, browser smoke and Worker deploy; Worker `b8dd91a4-4a0a-4d54-801e-e621c170a37e`, production smoke passed. The latest code retains the shared-window expiry retry and legal Peach `respond` UI. Full former repair instructions, failure traces and source analysis remain accessible in [immutable pre-closure §10.49](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/blob/69ff09d996a3dc461b1efe2a46d123c90bec3679/docs/UX2-refine.md), **not as an active execution queue**.

#### 10.49 Retained authoritative server architecture

- **One shared window and three modes:** The server owns `SharedResponseWindowPending` plus durable private `shared_response_window_submissions` for `ENTOURAGE_DODGE` (Cao Cao), `INFLUENCING_ATTACK` (Liu Bei Play phase and legal delegated response) and `DYING_PEACH` (rescue). Every window has an immutable **8-second** server deadline, unique ID, frozen eligible living players/priority, original causal root, valid continuation and one authorized settlement. Existing `NegationWindowPending`/`negation_window_submissions` and its 8-second counter-chain remain independent and unchanged.
- **Provisional private response; exactly one winner:** Eligible authenticated players independently send one valid `respond` intent with their own server-authorized provider/real material or Skip/Pass; frozen participants are all offered the same remaining countdown. Closing happens at the server deadline or early **only if every currently eligible frozen living player submitted**. Do NOT privilege network click order. At settlement validate player, root, faction, legal provider and genuine owned physical card again, then select **at most ONE still-valid player response per window**. The original single-response resolver runs once: only the selected payer's real card material is spent/played; losing intentions never deduct or reveal Hand cards, never create "refund", public CardFace, Hand-loss trigger or effects and are never carried to a later round. No intent/card/provider/count/locked state of a different viewer can leak publicly. CAS/lease/retry makes outcome, HP changes and public events idempotent; invalid/stale/forged/wrong actor/window/card/provider and late requests fail closed.
- **Cao Cao 护驾:** Living Wei helpers other than Cao Cao may privately offer a real legal Dodge/converted material or Skip. Choose the still-valid offer at **minimum raw living-seat-ring distance** to Cao Cao, with ties in forward living-seat order after Cao Cao (never submission timestamp or attack-distance equipment). Cao Cao remains the original attacked target/resolver, winning Wei the actual physical payer/author; only genuine fully proven Attack/Dodge success may announce full block, including multi-Dodge requirements such as Lü Bu. Zero winner returns Cao Cao to the remaining legal defense choice, **not** assumed damage.
- **Liu Bei 激将:** Living Shu helpers other than Liu Bei may simultaneously offer real legal Attack/converted material or Skip, including BOTH active Play and delegated response skill entries. Winning valid intent uses original **forward living-seat order after Liu Bei**, not nearest/delivery time. Liu Bei keeps semantic source, legal target/range and Attack-use limit, the chosen Shu helper keeps actual physical card author/cost. Preserve Guan Yu and Zhao Yun conversions and Serpent Spear's TWO material cards as ONE valid Attack response, the real Attack continuation and Duel/Group/other responses. Zero winner follows the original legal no-Attack/decline continuation.
- **濒死求桃:** All normally eligible living players (including dying target when legal) may privately choose ONE legal Peach or Hua Tuo First Aid response or Skip in each window. Winner is first still-valid Peach in the original rescue-seat order. Only the selected genuine Peach enters the **existing one-card recovery/HP/Hand-loss/trigger continuation**; preserve Sun Quan's conditional Wu-helper bonus and finish all nested recovery/optional skills BEFORE checking actual HP. If still HP≤0, create a **NEW** 8-second window with fresh ID and optional selections; if HP>0, resume normal continuation; if no valid Peach, follow true defeat once. Never apply multiple submitted Peaches in one window, retain loser queue, auto-resubmit old losers, invent HP or consume an unchosen donor card. Support already persisted legacy sequential Entourage/Influencing/Dying pending.

#### 10.49 Retained expiry, private Console and public H4/PAC

- **One public authoritative deadline, not a client auto-Skip:** Proven public `presentationV2.sharedResponseWindow` or verified snapshot provides mode/root/stage/windowId/deadline to all viewers, independent of TEST SWITCH and private actions. After expiry, send bounded idempotent `advance_timers`, refresh authoritative GET, and retry with backoff **only while the identical expired window remains current**. An in-flight/coalesced GET returning `false` is inconclusive and **must retry**, never conclude the window closed. CAS settles once, even with reconnect/two viewers. Never issue expired automatic `decline_response`/`skip_rescue`/`start_response_timer` in a shared window, accept a late intent or mutate from GET. Existing ordinary single-actor timers, Negation and other card flows retain their original behavior.
- **Private legal Confirm/Skip:** `CurrentAction` is the single authority for per-viewer eligible cards, conversion, one real Confirm/Skip action and locked acknowledgment. In `DYING_PEACH`, a server-eligible genuine Peach selection enables **Confirm** and submits private `respond` with actual `providerId/cardId`; no fake `give_peach` permission. Hua Tuo's eligible red material is offered only by the true `AS PEACH` provider. Preserve `give_peach` for legacy saved sequential rescue. Once the intent is acknowledged, show personal locked state, but **no optimistic public played card** before settlement. Invalid provider/no Peach may only Skip; hidden other-player Hand stays private.
- **Truthful H4/PAC and original physical CardFace:** H4 is the latest **committed proven PUBLIC fact**; PAC is the current neutral PUBLIC waiting/next state; Console is the authenticated viewer's PRIVATE action. While collecting, PAC conveys `Waiting for Wei Dodge responses for [CaoCaoPlayer].`, `Waiting for Shu Attack responses for [LiuBeiPlayer].`, or `Waiting for Peach responses to save [DyingPlayer].`. Do not publicly disclose intent/availability/submitter or claim a card was played before selection. On genuine winner commitment, H4 names the **real PLAYER contributor** and truthful Dodge/block, Attack/target, or Peach/actual recovered HP, with the real next PAC/Console stage. Show one original physical CardFace with printed rank/suit/art and authentic `AS DODGE`/`AS ATTACK`/`AS PEACH` conversion, centre display normally ≤~4s interruptible then park at **actual author Seat/Dock**, not semantic requester; no fake second card, improper visibility or H4/PAC overlap. Preserve accepted §10.45 geometry, §10.52 PAC semantics and §10.53 H4; unrelated pending §10.53-F1/Inspector/Huang Gai PUBLIC skill-event work is **not accepted by this decision**.

#### 10.49 Retained regression and future review gate

Preserve 4/5/6-seat priority and tie, all-replied early resolution versus a silent last actor at the exact shared 8s deadline, **Liu Bei valid Attack at 8s without 0s freeze**, **Huang Gai real Peach Confirm `respond` and one authentic recovery without wrongful defeat**, and **Cao Cao genuine helper Dodge and original block**. Validate genuine material ownership, optional conversions/Hua Tuo/Sun Quan, multi-round negative-HP rescue and nested skills, empty response/Skip, other-player privacy/Hand conservation, stale/duplicate/out-of-order POST, coalesced/503 GET recovery, lease retry/409 and legacy pending compatibility. Preserve existing Negation, sequential Duel and per-target group response authority. The owner accepted the above **three real-phone flows**, not an exhaustive browser matrix. A future bounded Coding Agent task gets **≤5 minutes total local browser/UI test budget**; focused unit/API/privacy and required CI/deployment are separate.

### 10.64 WORKER-1102-LONG-SESSION-RESOURCE-01 — delivered repeated-work optimization (B IMPLEMENTED / DEPLOYED)

#### 10.64-B — reduce proven repeated work while preserving fresh state

1. Derive the public event projection from the current authenticated viewer timeline, preserving the original public-event filters and sequence order. A viewer-private card event may remain visible to its owner but must never enter public facts, public card effects, interaction context or another viewer's projection.
2. Compute optional Attack/Dodge diagnostics only for the final fresh projection of an authenticated request with a valid `uxTraceId`. Keep the trace opt-in and preserve its existing public proof content and privacy boundaries.
3. Preserve the second full room projection after the discard-handoff step. It is the current freshness boundary for committed or concurrent state changes; do not remove it without a server-owned freshness proof and regression evidence for concurrent GET/POST and once-only handoff behavior.
4. Keep gameplay rules, response timers, polling recovery/backoff, POST behavior, authorization and viewer-specific data unchanged. Add focused assertions for equal public projections, owner-only private events, retained public roots and opt-in diagnostics. Exact-SHA CI, deployment and production smoke are required; none of them alone proves the 1102 incident is resolved.

This is **not** acceptance of the separately registered [EPIC-CARD-FLIGHT-01](EPIC-CARD-FLIGHT-01.md) or any change to the existing accepted §10.10 no-second-target-result rule.

**Retained product and implementation contract:**
1. **Authoritative turn scope:** Only a server-proven completed turn/handoff retires the **outgoing turn's temporary public presentation together** — H4 announcement from all shared/skill/discard owners, old PAC, transient countdown, central and moving public CardFaces, temporary result/settlement/COST/EFFECT groups, former player's played-card/response/Duel PARKED miniatures and obsolete public journey ownership. No old H4/PAC/card animation is retained on the following active turn. A local `END` tap, client timer, changing phase label, or `turnSeat` alone must not masquerade as a completed turn; consecutive turns for the same seat are distinct. Keep authenticated room/viewer scope, proven chronological event/turn identity and stale-response/reconnect/TEST SWITCH anti-replay rules. Do not erase historical game events, card IDs, or authoritative outcomes.
2. **Final Discard and unresolved-continuation exception:** A genuinely committed ordinary end-turn `discard_cards` retains its matching truthful H4 and original grey/white Discard card group **together** while the accepted §10.40 finite server-owned Discard handoff is still pending. Both retire when the handoff reaches the next authoritative active turn. A zero-card end produces no invented Discard. Mandatory end-turn Hero trigger, Dying, response, counter and pending server continuation are **not completed turns**; do not prematurely hide their legitimate H4/PAC/card or private decision. No new delays or changes to server action legality, timers or phase progression.
3. **Next turn and persistence:** The new turn immediately shows only its own proven PAC (e.g., actual new turn actor) and legitimate new H4/CardFace after **new** committed events; the private Local Console continues to show only this viewer's server-authorized legal actions. A brief empty public stage is safer than stale outgoing-turn text. Never clear genuine persistent Equipment/Weapon/Armor/Horse, attached Judgement, authenticated Hand, active server-owned Harvest continuation, Inspector/Seat state or genuine game-finished/winner result. Real physical card conservation and private identity protection remain exact.
4. **Visual continuity:** Keep accepted within-turn ~4s public CardFace first-visible read, distinct card/result-specific reads, actual confirmed-response interruption, ~380ms author parking, stable H4/PAC geometry, compact verified-name typography, and one physical public card owner. Turn-boundary retirement is a firm upper bound **for the previous turn's temporary views**, not a new global fixed timeout. Do not blank real ongoing scenes during polling or reanimate old events because of viewer/viewport change, slow/reordered GET or remount.
5. **Regression boundary:** End a turn after Attack/Dodge and verify outgoing H4/PAC/full CardFace/played miniatures are all absent at next active turn; real end-turn Discard remains visible during the valid handoff then clears as a unit; pending Dying/response/mandatory skills are not cleared early; same-seat consecutive turns, refresh/reconnect/TEST SWITCH/stale polls do not resurrect old presentation. Actual Equipment/Judgement/Hand and next player's PAC/Console stay correct. Continue accepted §§10.40/10.41/10.45/10.54/10.59/10.65, server authority, privacy and current 4–6-player new-room scope.

**Technical/acceptance provenance:** Delivered source `bb7819db2ed54d03511bde0caba24856a270c711`; CI follow-up `d189fc18446da8bda6904df08a067525cf078173` passed [exact-SHA GitHub Actions 44969910626](https://github.com/dmoneyUK/three-kingdoms/actions/runs/44969910626) with API, fast/lint, browser smoke, D1 migration, deployment and production smoke. Coding Agent HANDOVER reported Worker `f5e1758f-24fc-4773-a7f5-c445296f8704`, focused 440px local browser PASS, and 452/452 fast tests; local `app/page.tsx` ESLint hit a React Hooks stack overflow, while exact-SHA CI lint passed without weakening the rule. The owner's explicit PASS is the visual acceptance authority, not the CI result. ## 11. UX3 FUTURE backlog — Negation Plan B, immediate-public-on-Confirm (NOT AN UX2 TASK)

**User preference recorded 2026-10-14 (future UX3 only):** Explore individual immediate-public Negation Confirm through a separately approved future UX3 design. This is NOT authorized autonomous UX2 work. UX2 Negation Plan A remains private all-seat submission, maximum 8s and early server settlement only after EVERY living seat explicitly responds.

**Non-trivial unresolved tradeoff:** UX2 private simultaneous intents still use canonical seat priority; all-seats explicit early completion doesn't change that. Immediately displaying an INDIVIDUAL's Confirm as a PUBLIC card, while others remain undecided, could expose a losing intent, consume a wrong card or leak secret availability. UX3 requires a separately approved authoritative arbitration rule, not an optimistic animation or simply changing the 8s maximum.

**UX3 questions to design with user before authorization:** (1) first-arrival commitment versus stable clockwise seat priority / override grace window; (2) protecting opponent Negation availability and private Hand when no card is submitted; (3) new counter windows, timeouts, fairness under network lag and reconnect; (4) atomic exactly-once actual card consumption, conflicting simultaneous confirms, Duel/Group/Judgement consistency; (5) visible chronological author-attached card history, animation interruption, old-save migration, rollbacks and authenticated multi-viewer privacy tests.

**Separate UX3 design decision, not an UX2 review gate:** UX3 needs distinct product/rules authorization because it changes priority, privacy and fairness. Continue all approved UX2 work without awaiting acceptance of prior UX2 items, but do not silently implement this unapproved UX3 rule.

This appendix is **NOT the current work queue**. The current Tables A/B/C and latest explicit owner verdict under each feature govern present work; removed historical text does not reopen accepted features or change any rule.
